- **revive-card**: Restore a killed idea card back to active state. Moves the card from `ideas/killed/&lt;run-id&gt;/&lt;slug&gt;.md` to `ideas/&lt;run-id&gt;/&lt;slug&gt;.md`, resets the frontmatter to a safe baseline (`status: triaged`, kill-specific fields cleared), and appends a `card-revive` audit-log entry. Optionally also revives any orphaned MVP/V1 brief at `&lt;stack&gt;-apps/&lt;slug&gt;/`. The canonical undo path for kills made during `/validate-card`, `/scope-mvp`, `/scope-v1`, or `/discover`'s automatic triage. - **revive-project**: Oracle-Aware Revival Mode. Determine whether a stale, abandoned, or uncertain project still contains live value, and find the smallest experiment that proves it — instead of rebuilding on optimism. Use for: deciding whether to resurrect a dead branch, an abandoned prototype, a stalled research direction, or a "maybe still useful" repo. Triggers: /revive-project, "is this project worth reviving", "should we resurrect this", "is there still value here". NOT for: a project that is clearly still active, or a known rewrite decision already made. - **rhombus-clients**: List all Rhombus client organizations managed by the current partner account, with active/inactive state and a count of cameras per client. ([RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/partner/commands/rhombus-clients.md))
- **rhombus-context-refresh**: Regenerate the local Rhombus deployment context (index.md + manifest.json + per-camera stills) in `~/.rhombus/context/&lt;profile&gt;/`. Side-effects — downloads media and writes to disk. Slash-only. ([RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/user/commands/rhombus-context-refresh.md))
- **rhombus-curl**: Generate a ready-to-run cURL command for a Rhombus API endpoint, including auth headers and a skeleton request body. Only use this when the user needs a shell example rather than actual execution. ([RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/developer/commands/rhombus-curl.md))
- **rhombus-find-endpoint**: Search the Rhombus OpenAPI spec for endpoints by keyword, tag, or operationId. Returns matching operationId, path, method, and summary. ([RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/developer/commands/rhombus-find-endpoint.md))
- **rhombus-fleet-report**: Generate a shareable cross-client fleet report — weekly health, monthly executive, or incident rollup. Uses the rhombus-cross-client-reporting skill. ([RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/partner/commands/rhombus-fleet-report.md))
- **rhombus-mcp-status**: Report the status of the Rhombus MCP servers (rhombus-node-mcp and rhombus-docs). Diagnose connection issues and missing environment variables. ([RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/developer/commands/rhombus-mcp-status.md))
- **rhombus-newproject**: Scaffold a new Rhombus integration project with a typed SDK, webhook receiver, and README. Composes the rhombus-sdk-codegen and rhombus-webhook-receiver skills. ([RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/developer/commands/rhombus-newproject.md))
- **rhombus-schema**: Dump the request and response schema for a specific Rhombus API endpoint as markdown. Use when you need to know exactly what fields an endpoint accepts or returns. ([RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/developer/commands/rhombus-schema.md))
- **rhombus-sdk**: Generate a typed Rhombus SDK client from the official OpenAPI spec for Python, TypeScript, Java, or Go. Runs openapi-generator-cli. This is a side-effecting command that writes files to disk; keep it slash-only. ([RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/developer/commands/rhombus-sdk.md))
- **rhombus-status**: One-shot Rhombus deployment health report — cameras online/offline, door states, sensor battery levels, and recent device issues. ([RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/user/commands/rhombus-status.md))
- **rhombus-watch**: Open a Rhombus camera player in the browser. Side-effect — launches a browser window. Keep this slash-only so Claude doesn't trigger it autonomously. ([RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/RhombusSystems/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/user/commands/rhombus-watch.md))
- **rhyme_check**: Identify which historical era the current situation rhymes with, noting both key similarities and critical differences. ([peterzan/vantage-plugins](https://github.com/peterzan/vantage-plugins/blob/HEAD/history/commands/rhyme_check.md))
- **ri**: &gt; Shu-Ha-Ri frame · Stage: Transcend &gt; You are no longer thinking about individual components. &gt; You operate the whole. ([syncupsuite/webplatform4sync](https://github.com/syncupsuite/webplatform4sync/blob/HEAD/commands/ri.md) · ★1)
- **ri**: Read it — read content aloud in the DA's Fish voice. A URL, a file, pasted text, or bare "ri" for the last thing the DA wrote. Shortcut for the ReadIt skill. Bare "ri" means the same thing. ([danielmiessler/LifeOS](https://github.com/danielmiessler/LifeOS/blob/HEAD/LifeOS/install/commands/ri.md))
- **ria**: Ria (Omega GOV service line) ([HossamDaoud83/Omega_Plugins_Official](https://github.com/HossamDaoud83/Omega_Plugins_Official/blob/HEAD/plugins/gov/commands/ria.md))
- **riassumi**: Consolida l'output delle pipeline multi-agente — deduplica disclaimer, terminologia e citazioni con controllo della lunghezza (--breve/--medio/--lungo). ([fedec65/bettercallclaude_italia](https://github.com/fedec65/bettercallclaude_italia/blob/HEAD/bettercallclaude_italia/commands/riassumi.md) · ★54)
- **riassumi**: &gt; Summarize a Markdown file transcribed from PDF and save it in `Summaries/`. ([alessandro-vulcu-code/ICT2025](https://github.com/alessandro-vulcu-code/ICT2025/blob/HEAD/First%20Year/Second%20Semester/Stochastic%20Processes/.claude/commands/riassumi.md) · ★3)
- **riassumi**: Sei un esperto di Big Data Computing. Ti viene fornita la trascrizione di un documento del corso ($ARGUMENTS). Analizzala e produci un riassunto chiaro e conciso, senza essere prolisso, mantenendo il rigore tecnico di un corso universitario magistrale. ([alessandro-vulcu-code/ICT2025](https://github.com/alessandro-vulcu-code/ICT2025/blob/HEAD/First%20Year/Second%20Semester/Big%20Data%20Computing/.claude/commands/riassumi.md) · ★3)
- **riassumi**: &gt; Riassumi un file Markdown trascritto da PDF e salvalo in `Summaries/`. ([alessandro-vulcu-code/ICT2025](https://github.com/alessandro-vulcu-code/ICT2025/blob/HEAD/First%20Year/Second%20Semester/Multimedia%20Communications/.claude/commands/riassumi.md) · ★3)
- **riassumi-webapp**: Summarize and clean a Markdown note from Web Applications course. Use when the user invokes /riassumi, asks to riassumi/sintetizza/riorganizza a Markdown document, or wants an exam-ready summary of a lecture on servlets, JSP, REST, HTTP, HTML, CSS, JavaScript, SQL, Docker, Git, Maven, security, or web architecture. ([alessandro-vulcu-code/ICT2025](https://github.com/alessandro-vulcu-code/ICT2025/blob/HEAD/First%20Year/Second%20Semester/Web%20Applications/.claude/commands/riassumi.md) · ★3)

([laudtetteh/the-rig](https://github.com/laudtetteh/the-rig/blob/HEAD/templates/project/.claude/commands/rig-gaps.md) · ★1)
- **rig-help**: Print a reference table of all Rig slash commands with descriptions and key flags. ([laudtetteh/the-rig](https://github.com/laudtetteh/the-rig/blob/HEAD/templates/project/.claude/commands/rig-help.md) · ★1)
- **rig-profile**: You are documenting the hardware specifications for a machine. This profile will be used for compatibility analysis and upgrade recommendations. ([danielrosehill/procurement-tools-plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/procurement-tools-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/rig-profile.md) · ★2)
- **rig-propose**: Use this command when you believe a change to The Rig's governance files would improve the system. Governance files — `.rig/processes/`, `.rig/rules/`, `.husky/`, `CLAUDE.md`, and `.claude/hooks/` — are protected from direct writes by `pre-tool.sh`. ([laudtetteh/the-rig](https://github.com/laudtetteh/the-rig/blob/HEAD/templates/project/.claude/commands/rig-propose.md) · ★1)
- **rig-recommend**: You are generating specific component recommendations based on the user's hardware analysis, preferences, and budget. ([danielrosehill/procurement-tools-plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/procurement-tools-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/rig-recommend.md) · ★2)
- **rig-setup**: You are conducting the initial user interview to set up this hardware planning workspace. This is a structured interview process to gather all context needed for effective hardware upgrade planning. ([danielrosehill/procurement-tools-plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/procurement-tools-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/rig-setup.md) · ★2)
- **rig-status**: Show worker rig seats, loaded Ollama models and recent delegations ([gajeshbhat/agent-dreamteam](https://github.com/gajeshbhat/agent-dreamteam/blob/HEAD/commands/rig-status.md) · ★1)
- **rig-status**: Run a lightweight health check on The Rig's installation in this project. Pure diagnostic — no writes, no side effects. ([laudtetteh/the-rig](https://github.com/laudtetteh/the-rig/blob/HEAD/templates/project/.claude/commands/rig-status.md) · ★1)
- **rig-up**: Start the local open-model worker rig and attach this session as the boss ([gajeshbhat/agent-dreamteam](https://github.com/gajeshbhat/agent-dreamteam/blob/HEAD/commands/rig-up.md) · ★1)
- **rig-upgrade**: Upgrades The Rig in this project to the latest version available in the installer source. ([laudtetteh/the-rig](https://github.com/laudtetteh/the-rig/blob/HEAD/templates/project/.claude/commands/rig-upgrade.md) · ★1)
- **rig-upgrade**: Run The Rig upgrade workflow from the latest local installer source, not from a possibly stale project-installed command. ([laudtetteh/the-rig](https://github.com/laudtetteh/the-rig/blob/HEAD/templates/global/commands/rig-upgrade.md) · ★1)
- **rigging**: Manage the governed rules every build reads. `compact` derives the caller-facing usage surface of a specification into a `*_compact.md` sibling so consumer stories carry the contract, not the whole file. `update` and `verify` propagate and check business rules in a built Target. `--add` catalogs new Rigging files for Analyze. ([webcloudstudio/Drydock](https://github.com/webcloudstudio/Drydock/blob/HEAD/design/commands/rigging.md) · ★14)
- **riggr-guide**: Navigate RIGGR's current agents, skills, commands, hooks, install profiles, and docs from the live repository surface. This is the slash-command entrypoint for that navigation; the riggr-guide skill covers the same map for on-demand use in conversation. ([PankajPandey-codes/riggr](https://github.com/PankajPandey-codes/riggr/blob/HEAD/commands/riggr-guide.md) · ★1)
- **riggr-guide**: RIGGRの現在のエージェント、スキル、コマンド、フック、インストールプロファイル、ドキュメントをライブリポジトリサーフェスからナビゲートします。 ([PankajPandey-codes/riggr](https://github.com/PankajPandey-codes/riggr/blob/HEAD/docs/ja-JP/commands/riggr-guide.md) · ★1)

- **rigor**: This page is the complete command reference for the `rigor` plugin. It carries one entry per command: how it works, why it is useful, when to reach for it, and what it hands off. Read it when you are picking a verification command or wiring one into a larger run. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/code-ops-docs/40%20Engineering/Handbook/commands/rigor.md))
- **rigor-architecture-review**: Assess a repository's current architecture through read-only evidence gathering, parallel domain analysis, and prioritized findings ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-architecture-review.md))
- **rigor-brainstorm**: Socratic design exploration -- transforms rough ideas into validated designs through structured questioning, alternative exploration, and explicit approval gates ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-brainstorm.md))
- **rigor-bug-hunt**: Use to find REAL bugs, each proven with a failing test, not a list of guesses. The flagship. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-bug-hunt.md))
- **rigor-commit**: Commit changes with scope allowlist enforcement, atomic grouping, configurable GPG signing, configurable trailers, and conventional commits ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-commit.md))
- **rigor-cycle**: Drive a full development cycle through the Rigor MCP gate server: init from a plan, start/complete tasks with Gate 0 enforcement, submit reviews (Gate 8), accept epics (Gate 9), and advance phases ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-cycle.md))
- **rigor-debug**: Systematic 4-phase debugging -- investigate, trace root cause backward through call chain, hypothesize and test, then minimal fix with regression test ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-debug.md))
- **rigor-deep-review**: Use for pre-merge review of a PR or diff. bar verified (default) blocks only on CONFIRMED defects; bar standard reviews every lens; unproven items are advisory. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-deep-review.md))
- **rigor-explore**: Two-phase autonomous codebase exploration -- discover structure with parallel agents, then deep-dive each discovered area with adaptive agents ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-explore.md))
- **rigor-fix-verified**: Use when CONFIRMED bugs exist and need root-cause fixes with proof. Requires CONFIRMED findings. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-fix-verified.md))
- **rigor-ground-truth**: Use first, for the factual baseline before analysis: runs the real toolchain, captures ground truth and a coverage and blind-spot map. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-ground-truth.md))
- **rigor-improve-measured**: Use for measured, behavior-preserving improvements, not speculative refactors. Profiling-led hot-path work goes to /code-ops-suite-performance. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-improve-measured.md))
- **rigor-init**: Project onboarding — detects the project's domain and tech stack, creates or audits .rigor/config.yaml with the correct domain pack and gate commands ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-init.md))
- **rigor-lint**: Run lint and fix issues — detects language, runs the lang pack's lint command, fixes issues directly, verifies clean ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-lint.md))
- **rigor-new-skill**: Scaffold a new Rigor skill: gather requirements, validate the name against naming conventions, classify the skill type, generate the SKILL.md with appropriate sections and TODO markers, and update the catalog ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-new-skill.md))
- **rigor-plan**: Write a rolling-wave phased implementation plan from a spec before coding: phase-epic-task hierarchy where Phase 1 is detailed into dispatch-ready tasks and later phases stay epic-level for elaboration during execution ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-plan.md))
- **rigor-pr**: Open a GitHub Pull Request with automatic base branch detection (3 probes), scope allowlist enforcement, PR template filling, precondition verification, and post-create base branch verification with retargeting ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-pr.md))
- **rigor-quality-scan**: Use when you want high-signal, defect-causing quality issues with evidence and tiers, not cosmetic nits. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-quality-scan.md))
- **rigor-receive-review**: Process code review feedback with technical verification — read, verify, evaluate, then implement one at a time ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-receive-review.md))
- **rigor-regression-hunt**: Use when something used to work: pinpoints the breaking commit and related regressions in recent changes. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-regression-hunt.md))
- **rigor-review**: Dispatch parallel code reviewers over a git diff with hybrid deterministic + AI analysis ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-review.md))
- **rigor-safety-net**: Use before refactoring or fixing low-coverage code; writes characterization tests that lock current behavior. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-safety-net.md))
- **rigor-ship**: End-to-end git orchestrator: branch, commit, push, PR, with a full plan presented before any execution ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-ship.md))
- **rigor-test-guard**: Test quality guard — catches mock abuse, test-only production methods, and testing anti-patterns before they enter the codebase ([caioaletroca/rigor](https://github.com/caioaletroca/rigor/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/rigor-test-guard.md))

- **rm**: Removes tracked files or directories from the index and, by default, from the working tree. - **rm**: Remove files from the working tree and/or the index. ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/rm.md) · ★101)
